232|     (function () {
233|         'use strict';
234| 
235|         // Lecture des media queries CSS (vous les modifiez dans le CSS)
236|         const desktopMQ = window.matchMedia('(min-width: 1081px)');
237|         const mobileMQ = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 350px)');
238|         // Entre 351px et 1080px = mode desktop également
239| 
240|         // === VERSION DESKTOP ===
241|         function initDesktopVersion() {
242|             const nav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
243|             const panel = document.getElementById('navSettingsPanel');
244|             const settingsBtn = document.getElementById('navSettingsBtn');
245|             const navButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.mobile-nav button:not(#toggleNavBtn)');
246| 
247|             let isPanelOpen = false;
248|             let animationTimer = null;
249| 
250|             if (nav) nav.style.display = 'flex';
251| 
252|             // Bouton bleu
253|             const oldToggleBtn = document.getElementById('toggleNavBtn');
254|             if (oldToggleBtn) {
255|                 const newToggleBtn = oldToggleBtn.cloneNode(true);
256|                 oldToggleBtn.parentNode.replaceChild(newToggleBtn, oldToggleBtn);
257|                 newToggleBtn.onclick = function (e) {
258|                     e.preventDefault();
259|                     const isVisible = navButtons[0]?.classList.contains('hidden');
260|                     navButtons.forEach(btn => {
261|                         if (isVisible) btn.classList.remove('hidden');
262|                         else btn.classList.add('hidden');
263|                     });
264|                     localStorage.setItem('mobileNavVisible', !isVisible);
265|                 };
266|             }
267| 
268|             function setPanelOrientation() {
269|                 if (!panel || !nav) return;
270|                 const pos = nav.getAttribute('data-position') || 'bottom';
271|                 panel.classList.remove('horizontal', 'vertical');
272|                 if (pos === 'left' || pos === 'right') {
273|                     panel.classList.add('vertical');
274|                 } else {
275|                     panel.classList.add('horizontal');
276|                 }
277|             }
278| 
279|             function positionPanel() {
280|                 if (!panel || !nav) return;
281|                 const navRect = nav.getBoundingClientRect();
282|                 const panelW = panel.offsetWidth;
283|                 const panelH = panel.offsetHeight;
284|                 const pos = nav.getAttribute('data-position') || 'bottom';
285|                 if (panelW === 0 || panelH === 0) return;
286| 
287|                 let top, left;
288|                 switch (pos) {
289|                     case 'left':
290|                         left = navRect.right + 8;
291|                         top = navRect.top + (navRect.height / 2) - (panelH / 2);
292|                         break;
293|                     case 'right':
294|                         left = navRect.left - panelW - 8;
295|                         top = navRect.top + (navRect.height / 2) - (panelH / 2);
296|                         break;
297|                     case 'top':
298|                         left = navRect.left;
299|                         top = navRect.bottom + 8;
300|                         break;
301|                     default:
302|                         left = navRect.left;
303|                         top = navRect.top - panelH - 8;
304|                         break;
305|                 }
306| 
307|                 if (left < 5) left = 5;
308|                 if (left + panelW > window.innerWidth - 5) left = window.innerWidth - panelW - 5;
309|                 if (top < 5) top = 5;
310|                 if (top + panelH > window.innerHeight - 5) {
311|                     if (pos === 'top') top = navRect.top - panelH - 8;
312|                     else if (pos === 'bottom') top = navRect.bottom + 8;
313|                     else top = window.innerHeight - panelH - 5;
314|                 }
315| 
316|                 panel.style.top = top + 'px';
317|                 panel.style.left = left + 'px';
318|             }
319| 
320|             function openPanel() {
321|                 if (!panel) return;
322|                 panel.style.display = 'flex';
323|                 isPanelOpen = true;
324|                 setPanelOrientation();
325|                 positionPanel();
326|             }
327| 
328|             function closePanel() {
329|                 if (!panel) return;
330|                 panel.style.display = 'none';
331|                 isPanelOpen = false;
332|                 if (animationTimer) clearTimeout(animationTimer);
333|             }
334| 
335|             function togglePanel(e) {
336|                 e.stopPropagation();
337|                 if (isPanelOpen) closePanel();
338|                 else openPanel();
339|             }
340| 
341|             function setNavPosition(position) {
342|                 if (!nav) return;
343|                 nav.setAttribute('data-position', position);
344|                 localStorage.setItem('mobileNavPosition', position);
345|                 if (isPanelOpen) {
346|                     if (animationTimer) clearTimeout(animationTimer);
347|                     animationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
348|                         setPanelOrientation();
349|                         positionPanel();
350|                         animationTimer = null;
351|                     }, 320);
352|                 }
353|             }
354| 
355|             function loadSavedSettings() {
356|                 const savedPos = localStorage.getItem('mobileNavPosition');
357|                 if (savedPos && ['top', 'bottom', 'left', 'right'].includes(savedPos)) {
358|                     nav.setAttribute('data-position', savedPos);
359|                 } else {
360|                     nav.setAttribute('data-position', 'bottom');
361|                 }
362|                 const savedVis = localStorage.getItem('mobileNavVisible');
363|                 if (savedVis === 'true') navButtons.forEach(btn => btn.classList.add('hidden'));
364|                 else if (savedVis === 'false') navButtons.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('hidden'));
365|             }
366| 
367|             function resetAll() {
368|                 closePanel();
369|                 localStorage.removeItem('mobileNavPosition');
370|                 localStorage.removeItem('mobileNavVisible');
371|                 location.reload();
372|             }
373| 
374|             function handleClickOutside(e) {
375|                 if (!isPanelOpen) return;
376|                 if (!nav.contains(e.target) && !panel.contains(e.target)) closePanel();
377|             }
378| 
379|             function scrollToTarget(e) {
380|                 const btn = e.currentTarget;
381|                 const targetId = btn.getAttribute('data-target');
382|                 if (!targetId) return;
383|                 e.preventDefault();
384|                 const target = document.getElementById(targetId);
385|                 if (target) {
386|                     closePanel();
387|                     const offset = nav.getAttribute('data-position') === 'top' ? nav.offsetHeight + 20 : 80;
388|                     const pos = target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - offset;
389|                     window.scrollTo({ top: pos, behavior: 'smooth' });
390|                 }
391|             }
392| 
393|             if (nav && panel) {
394|                 loadSavedSettings();
395|                 if (settingsBtn) settingsBtn.onclick = togglePanel;
396|                 document.getElementById('navPosTop')?.addEventListener('click', () => setNavPosition('top'));
397|                 document.getElementById('navPosBottom')?.addEventListener('click', () => setNavPosition('bottom'));
398|                 document.getElementById('navPosLeft')?.addEventListener('click', () => setNavPosition('left'));
399|                 document.getElementById('navPosRight')?.addEventListener('click', () => setNavPosition('right'));
400|                 document.getElementById('navReset')?.addEventListener('click', resetAll);
401|                 document.querySelectorAll('[data-target]').forEach(btn => {
402|                     btn.addEventListener('click', scrollToTarget);
403|                 });
404|                 document.addEventListener('click', handleClickOutside);
405|                 window.addEventListener('resize', () => { if (isPanelOpen) positionPanel(); });
406|             }
407|         }
408| 
409|         // === VERSION MOBILE (très petits écrans) ===
410|         function initMobileVersion() {
411|             const oldNav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
412|             if (oldNav) oldNav.style.display = 'none';
413| 
414|             let menuBtn = document.getElementById('mobileMenuBtn');
415|             if (!menuBtn) {
416|                 menuBtn = document.createElement('button');
417|                 menuBtn.id = 'mobileMenuBtn';
418|                 menuBtn.innerHTML = '☰';
419|                 document.body.appendChild(menuBtn);
420|             }
421| 
422|             let panel = document.getElementById('mobileNavPanel');
423|             if (!panel) {
424|                 panel = document.createElement('div');
425|                 panel.id = 'mobileNavPanel';
426|                 panel.innerHTML = `
427|                 <div class="mobile-panel-buttons">
428|                     <button data-target="connectes">👥<span>Connectés</span></button>
429|                     <button data-target="chat">💬<span>Chat</span></button>
430|                     <button data-target="new-window">📦<span>Nouveau</span></button>
431|                     <button data-target="menus">📋<span>Menus</span></button>
432|                 </div>
433|             `;
434|                 document.body.appendChild(panel);
435|             }
436| 
437|             let isOpen = false;
438| 
439|             function openPanel() {
440|                 panel.classList.add('open');
441|                 isOpen = true;
442|             }
443| 
444|             function closePanel() {
445|                 panel.classList.remove('open');
446|                 isOpen = false;
447|             }
448| 
449|             function togglePanel() {
450|                 if (isOpen) closePanel();
451|                 else openPanel();
452|             }
453| 
454|             function scrollToTarget(e) {
455|                 const btn = e.currentTarget;
456|                 const targetId = btn.getAttribute('data-target');
457|                 if (!targetId) return;
458|                 e.preventDefault();
459|                 const target = document.getElementById(targetId);
460|                 if (target) {
461|                     const pos = target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - 70;
462|                     window.scrollTo({ top: pos, behavior: 'smooth' });
463|                 }
464|             }
465| 
466|             menuBtn.addEventListener('click', togglePanel);
467|             document.querySelectorAll('#mobileNavPanel [data-target]').forEach(btn => {
468|                 btn.addEventListener('click', scrollToTarget);
469|             });
470| 
471|             document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
472|                 if (isOpen && !panel.contains(e.target) && e.target !== menuBtn) {
473|                     closePanel();
474|                 }
475|             });
476|         }
477| 
478|         // === LANCEMENT (basé sur les media queries CSS) ===
479|         function detectAndInit() {
480|             if (mobileMQ.matches) {
481|                 initMobileVersion();
482|             } else {
483|                 initDesktopVersion();
484|             }
485|         }
486| 
487|         detectAndInit();
488| 
489|         // === REDIMENSIONNEMENT ===
490|         let resizeTimer;
491|         desktopMQ.addEventListener('change', function () {
492|             if (resizeTimer) clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
493|             resizeTimer = setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 150);
494|         });
495|         mobileMQ.addEventListener('change', function () {
496|             if (resizeTimer) clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
497|             resizeTimer = setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 150);
498|         });
499|     })();
500| 
501| })();

56| // Mettre à jour la liste des bloqués dans le panel
57| function updateBlockedList() {
58|     const list = document.getElementById('blockedList');
59|     const count = document.getElementById('blockedCount');
60|     if (!list) return;
61| 
62|     const blocked = window.blockedUsers || [];
63|     if (count) count.textContent = blocked.length;
64| 
65|     if (blocked.length === 0) {
66|         list.innerHTML = 'Aucun utilisateur bloqué';
67|         return;
68|     }
69| 
70|     list.innerHTML = blocked.map(pseudo => `
71|         <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; padding: 3px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #333;">
72|             <span>${escapeHtml(pseudo)}</span>
73|             <span style="color: #ff6b6b; cursor: pointer;" onclick="unblockUser('${pseudo}')">Débloquer</span>
74|         </div>
75|     `).join('');
76| }

78| // Charger la liste des bloqués depuis la base
79| async function loadBlockedUsers() {
80|     console.log('🔍 loadBlockedUsers appelée, user.pseudo =', user?.pseudo);  // ← LOG
81|     try {
82|         const response = await fetch('/api/blocked-list', {
83|             method: 'POST',
84|             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
85|             body: JSON.stringify({ pseudo: user.pseudo })
86|         });
87|         const data = await response.json();
88|         console.log('📥 Réponse de /api/blocked-list:', data);  // ← LOG
89|         if (data.success) {
90|             window.blockedUsers = data.blocked || [];
91|             console.log('🔒 Bloques chargés depuis la base:', window.blockedUsers);
92|             updateBlockedList();
93|             window.fetchActiveUsers();
94|         }
95|     } catch (err) {
96|         console.error('❌ Erreur chargement bloqués:', err);
97|         window.blockedUsers = [];
98|     }
99| }
100| 
101| // Bloquer un utilisateur
102| window.blockUser = async function (pseudo) {
103|     console.log('🔥 blockUser appelée pour', pseudo);
104| 
105|     if (pseudo === user.pseudo) {
106|         alert('Vous ne pouvez pas vous bloquer vous-même.');
107|         return;
108|     }
109| 
110|     if (window.blockedUsers.includes(pseudo)) {
111|         alert(`${pseudo} est déjà bloqué.`);
112|         return;
113|     }
114| 
115|     try {
116|         console.log('📤 Appel à /api/block avec:', { blocker: user.pseudo, blocked: pseudo });
117|         const response = await fetch('/api/block', {
118|             method: 'POST',
119|             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
120|             body: JSON.stringify({ blocker: user.pseudo, blocked: pseudo })
121|         });
122|         const data = await response.json();
123|         console.log('📥 Réponse de /api/block:', data);
124| 
125|         if (data.success) {
126|             window.blockedUsers.push(pseudo);
127|             updateBlockedList();
128|             if (currentTargetName === pseudo) {
129|                 messageInput.disabled = true;
130|                 sendBtn.disabled = true;
131|                 messageInput.placeholder = `🔇 ${pseudo} est bloqué. Débloquez-le dans les réglages.`;
132|             }
133|             window.fetchActiveUsers();
134|             console.log(`🔒 ${pseudo} bloqué`);
135|         } else {
136|             // 🔥 NOUVEAU : Même si l'API retourne une erreur "Déjà bloqué", on met à jour l'interface
137|             if (data.error === 'Déjà bloqué') {
138|                 if (!window.blockedUsers.includes(pseudo)) {
139|                     window.blockedUsers.push(pseudo);
140|                     updateBlockedList();
141|                     if (currentTargetName === pseudo) {
142|                         messageInput.disabled = true;
143|                         sendBtn.disabled = true;
144|                         messageInput.placeholder = `🔇 ${pseudo} est bloqué. Débloquez-le dans les réglages.`;
145|                     }
146|                     window.fetchActiveUsers();
147|                     console.log(`🔒 ${pseudo} déjà bloqué (interface synchronisée)`);
148|                 }
149|             } else {
150|                 alert(data.error || 'Erreur lors du blocage');
151|             }
152|         }
153|     } catch (err) {
154|         console.error('❌ Erreur blocage:', err);
155|         alert('Erreur lors du blocage');
156|     }
157| };
158| 
159| // Débloquer un utilisateur
160| window.unblockUser = async function (pseudo) {
161|     console.log('🔥 unblockUser appelée pour', pseudo);
162|     try {
163|         const response = await fetch('/api/unblock', {
164|             method: 'POST',
165|             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
166|             body: JSON.stringify({ blocker: user.pseudo, blocked: pseudo })
167|         });
168|         const data = await response.json();
169|         if (data.success) {
170|             const index = window.blockedUsers.indexOf(pseudo);
171|             if (index > -1) {
172|                 window.blockedUsers.splice(index, 1);
173|             }
174|             updateBlockedList();
175|             // Si c'était le contact actuel, réactiver l'envoi
176|             if (currentTargetName === pseudo) {
177|                 messageInput.disabled = false;
178|                 sendBtn.disabled = false;
179|                 messageInput.placeholder = `Message pour ${pseudo}...`;
180|             }
181|             // Rafraîchir la liste des utilisateurs
182|             window.fetchActiveUsers();
183|             console.log(`🔓 ${pseudo} débloqué`);
184|         } else {
185|             alert(data.error || 'Erreur lors du déblocage');
186|         }
187|     } catch (err) {
188|         console.error('Erreur déblocage:', err);
189|         alert('Erreur lors du déblocage');
190|     }
191| };
192| 
193| // ========== SYSTÈME DE MODAL (remplace MGp......) ==========
194| (function () {
195|     function createModalStructure() {
196|         if (document.getElementById('mg-modal-container')) return;
197| 
198|         const modalHTML = `
199| <div id="mg-modal-container" style="display: none; position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.6); z-index: 10000; justify-content: center; align-items: center;">
200|     <div id="mg-modal-box" style="background: #1e1e2e; border-radius: 16px; padding: 24px; max-width: 380px; width: 85%; text-align: center; box-shadow: 0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); border: 1px solid #333; font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif; transform: translateY(-50px); opacity: 0; transition: all 0.3s ease;">
201|         <h3 id="mg-modal-title" style="margin: 0 0 12px 0; color: #ff6b6b; font-size: 1.3rem;">⚠️ Attention</h3>
202|         <p id="mg-modal-message" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0; color: #ccc; font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.5;">Message par défaut</p>
203|         <div style="display: flex; gap: 12px; justify-content: center;">
204|             <button id="mg-modal-cancel" style="background: #3a3a4a; border: none; padding: 10px 20px; border-radius: 8px; color: #ccc; cursor: pointer; font-size: 0.9rem;">Annuler</button>
205|             <button id="mg-modal-confirm" style="background: #ff6b6b; border: none; padding: 10px 20px; border-radius: 8px; color: white; cursor: pointer; font-size: 0.9rem;">OK</button>
206|         </div>
207|     </div>
208| </div>
209| `;
210| 
211|         document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', modalHTML);
212|     }

214|     function setupButtons(onConfirm, onCancel) {
215|         const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('mg-modal-confirm');
216|         const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('mg-modal-cancel');
217|         const container = document.getElementById('mg-modal-container');
218| 
219|         if (confirmBtn) {
220|             const newConfirm = confirmBtn.cloneNode(true);
221|             confirmBtn.parentNode.replaceChild(newConfirm, confirmBtn);
222|             newConfirm.onclick = () => {
223|                 container.style.display = 'none';
224|                 if (onConfirm) onConfirm();
225|             };
226|         }
227| 
228|         if (cancelBtn) {
229|             const newCancel = cancelBtn.cloneNode(true);
230|             cancelBtn.parentNode.replaceChild(newCancel, cancelBtn);
231|             newCancel.onclick = () => {
232|                 container.style.display = 'none';
233|                 if (onCancel) onCancel();
234|             };
235|         }
236| 
237|         container.onclick = (e) => {
238|             if (e.target === container) {
239|                 container.style.display = 'none';
240|                 if (onCancel) onCancel();
241|             }
242|         };
243|     }
244| 
245|     window.MGModal = {
246|         show: function (options) {
247|             createModalStructure();
248| 
249|             const container = document.getElementById('mg-modal-container');
250|             const box = document.getElementById('mg-modal-box');
251|             const titleEl = document.getElementById('mg-modal-title');
252|             const messageEl = document.getElementById('mg-modal-message');
253| 
254|             titleEl.textContent = options.title || '⚠️ Attention';
255|             messageEl.textContent = options.message || 'Message par défaut';
256| 
257|             setupButtons(options.onConfirm, options.onCancel);
258| 
259|             container.style.display = 'flex';
260| 
261|             setTimeout(() => {
262|                 if (box) {
263|                     box.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
264|                     box.style.opacity = '1';
265|                 }
266|             }, 10);
267|         },
268| 
269|         hide: function () {
270|             const container = document.getElementById('mg-modal-container');
271|             if (container) container.style.display = 'none';
272|         }
273|     };
274| })();

298| let reconnectAttempts = 0;
299| const MAX_RECONNECT_ATTEMPTS = 10;
300| const RECONNECT_DELAYS = [500, 1000, 2000, 4000, 8000, 16000, 30000, 30000, 30000, 30000];
301| 
302| function reconnect() {
303|     if (reconnectAttempts >= MAX_RECONNECT_ATTEMPTS) return;
304|     const delay = RECONNECT_DELAYS[reconnectAttempts] || 30000;
305|     reconnectAttempts++;
306|     setTimeout(() => {
307|         socket.connect();
308|     }, delay);
309| }
310| 
311| socket.on('disconnect', () => {
312|     reconnect();
313| });
314| 
315| socket.on('connect', () => {
316|     reconnectAttempts = 0;
317| });
318| 
319| // ========== GESTION DE LA RECONNEXION APRÈS VEILLE ==========
320| function checkAndReconnect() {
321|     if (socket) {
322|         if (!socket.connected) {
323|             console.log('🔄 Socket déconnecté, reconnexion forcée...');
324|             socket.connect();
325|             // Après reconnexion, vérifier les messages
326|             setTimeout(() => {
327|                 if (socket.connected) {
328|                     const pseudo = getCurrentPseudo();
329|                     if (pseudo) {
330|                         console.log('📨 Vérification des messages en attente après reconnexion forcée');
331|                         socket.emit('check-pending', { pseudo: pseudo });
332|                     }
333|                 }
334|             }, 1000);
335|         } else {
336|             // Si connecté, vérifier quand même (par sécurité)
337|             const pseudo = getCurrentPseudo();
338|             if (pseudo) {
339|                 console.log('📨 Vérification périodique des messages en attente');
340|                 socket.emit('check-pending', { pseudo: pseudo });
341|             }
342|         }
343|     }
344| }

349|         MGModal.show({
350|             title: '⚠️ Inactivité',
351|             message: data.message,
352|             onConfirm: () => {
353|                 // L'utilisateur a cliqué → réinitialiser
354|                 socket.emit('reset-inactivity');
355|             }
356|         });
357|     } else {
358|         // Fallback
359|         alert(data.message);
360|         socket.emit('reset-inactivity');
361|     }
362| });

368|     }
369| };

442| // ========== RÉCUPÉRATION DES INFOS UTILISATEUR DEPUIS LA BASE ==========00000
443| let user = null;
444| let currentTargetName = null;
445| const storedUser = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('meetgay_user'));
446| 
447| async function initUser() {
448|     console.log('1. Début initUser');
449| 
450|     // 🔥 NOUVEAU : Vérifier si c'est un rechargement
451|     const isReloading = sessionStorage.getItem('meetgay_is_reloading') === 'true';
452|     if (isReloading) {
453|         console.log('🔄 Rechargement détecté, attente avant de continuer...');
454|         // Attendre que la page soit stabilisée
455|         await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 800));
456|         console.log('✅ Reprise après attente');
457|     }
458| 
459|     if (!storedUser || !storedUser.pseudo) {
460|         console.log('2. Pas de storedUser');
461|         // Ne rediriger que si ce n'est pas un rechargement
462|         if (!isReloading) {
463|             window.location.href = '/login.html';
464|         }
465|         return;
466|     }
467| 
468|     try {
469|         console.log('3. Appel fetch pour pseudo:', storedUser.pseudo);
470|         const response = await fetch('/api/get-user', {
471|             method: 'POST',
472|             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
473|             body: JSON.stringify({ pseudo: storedUser.pseudo })
474|         });
475| 
476|         console.log('4. Réponse reçue, status:', response.status);
477|         const data = await response.json();
478|         console.log('5. Data reçue:', data);
479| 
480|         if (data.success) {
481|             user = data.user;
482|             currentUserPseudo = user.pseudo;
483|             localStorage.setItem('meetgay_user_full', JSON.stringify(user));
484|             console.log('6. Utilisateur chargé, démarrage chat');
485|             startChat();
486|         } else {
487|             console.log('7. Pas de success');
488|             // 🔥 SI C'EST UN RECHARGEMENT ET QUE L'UTILISATEUR N'EXISTE PAS
489|             if (isReloading) {
490|                 console.log('⚠️ Utilisateur non trouvé pendant rechargement - Tentative de récupération');
491|                 // Essayer de récupérer l'utilisateur via une autre méthode
492|                 try {
493|                     const reconnResponse = await fetch('/api/reconnect-user', {
494|                         method: 'POST',
495|                         headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
496|                         body: JSON.stringify({ pseudo: storedUser.pseudo })
497|                     });
498|                     const reconnData = await reconnResponse.json();
499|                     if (reconnData.success) {
500|                         user = reconnData.user;
501|                         currentUserPseudo = user.pseudo;
502|                         localStorage.setItem('meetgay_user_full', JSON.stringify(user));
503|                         console.log('✅ Utilisateur récupéré, démarrage chat');
504|                         startChat();
505|                         return;
506|                     }
507|                 } catch (e) {
508|                     console.error('Erreur reconnexion:', e);
509|                 }
510|             }
511|             // Si tout échoue, rediriger
512|             window.location.href = '/login.html';
513|         }
514|     } catch (err) {
515|         console.error('Erreur:', err);
516|         if (!isReloading) {
517|             window.location.href = '/login.html';
518|         }
519|     }
520| }

522| // ========== DÉMARRAGE DU CHAT (après récupération des infos) ==========
523| function startChat() {
524| 
525| 
526|     if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
527|         navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').then(reg => {
528|             console.log('SW enregistré');
529|             if (reg.active) {
530|                 reg.active.postMessage({ action: 'userConnected', connected: true });
531|             } else {
532|                 reg.installing?.postMessage({ action: 'userConnected', connected: true });
533|             }
534|         });
535|     }
536|     // Charger l'historique des messages pour une conversation
537|     async function loadHistory(withPseudo) {
538|         try {
539|             const response = await fetch(`/api/messages/${encodeURIComponent(user.pseudo)}/${encodeURIComponent(withPseudo)}`);
540|             const messages = await response.json();
541| 
542|             // Nettoyer l'historique existant pour ce contact
543|             if (!conversationHistory[withPseudo]) {
544|                 conversationHistory[withPseudo] = [];
545|             } else {
546|                 conversationHistory[withPseudo] = [];
547|             }
548| 
549|             // Ajouter les messages chargés
550|             messages.forEach(msg => {
551|                 const type = msg.from_pseudo === user.pseudo ? 'sent' : 'received';
552|                 conversationHistory[withPseudo].push({
553|                     text: msg.message,
554|                     type: type,
555|                     from: msg.from_pseudo,
556|                     to: msg.to_pseudo,
557|                     timestamp: new Date(msg.created_at)
558|                 });
559|             });
560| 
561|             updateHistoryDisplay();
562|         } catch (err) {
563|             console.error('Erreur chargement historique:', err);
564|         }
565|     }
566| 
567|     const usersDiv = document.getElementById('usersList');
568|     const messageInput = document.getElementById('messageInput');
569|     const sendBtn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
570|     const envelope = document.getElementById('envelope');
571| 
572| 
573|     // --------------------------------------------------------------
574|     // GESTION DE LA LISTE DES CONNECTÉS
575|     // --------------------------------------------------------------
576| 
577|     window.fetchActiveUsers = async function () {
578|         try {
579|             const response = await fetch('/api/users/active');
580|             const usersListData = await response.json();
581| 
582|             usersListData.sort((a, b) => (b.first_connected_at || 0) - (a.first_connected_at || 0));
583|             usersList = usersListData;
584| 
585|             const onlineCount = document.getElementById('onlineCount');
586|             if (onlineCount) {
587|                 onlineCount.textContent = usersList.length;
588|             }
589| 
590|             if (usersList.length === 0) {
591|                 usersDiv.innerHTML = '<em>Aucun connecté</em>';
592|                 return;
593|             }
594| 
595|             usersDiv.innerHTML = '';
596| 
597|             for (const fullUser of usersList) {
598|                 const div = document.createElement('div');
599|                 div.className = 'user-item';
600|                 const orientationSymbol = getOrientationSymbol(fullUser.tendencies);
601|                 const isBlocked = window.blockedUsers.includes(fullUser.pseudo);
602|                 const locationDisplay = fullUser.location_city || fullUser.location_code || '?';
603| 
604|                 if (fullUser.pseudo === user.pseudo) {
605|                     div.innerHTML = `<strong>${escapeHtml(fullUser.pseudo)}</strong> <span style="font-size:12px;">(${fullUser.age} ans, ${orientationSymbol} ${locationDisplay})</span>`;
606|                     div.style.background = '#a8e3ee';
607|                     div.style.fontStyle = 'italic';
608|                 } else {
609|                     div.innerHTML = `<strong>${escapeHtml(fullUser.pseudo)}</strong> <span style="font-size:12px;">(${fullUser.age} ans, ${orientationSymbol} ${locationDisplay})</span>`;
610|                     if (isBlocked) {
611|                         div.style.background = '#ffcccc';
612|                     }
613| 
614|                     div.onclick = () => {
615|                         sessionStorage.setItem('last_contact', fullUser.pseudo);
616|                         document.querySelectorAll('.user-item').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('current-target'));
617|                         div.classList.add('current-target');
618| 
619|                         currentTarget = fullUser.pseudo;
620|                         currentTargetName = fullUser.pseudo;
621| 
622|                         if (window.blockedUsers.includes(fullUser.pseudo)) {
623|                             messageInput.disabled = true;
624|                             sendBtn.disabled = true;
625|                             messageInput.placeholder = `🔇 ${fullUser.pseudo} est bloqué. Cliquez sur 🚫 pour débloquer.`;
626|                         } else {
627|                             messageInput.disabled = false;
628|                             sendBtn.disabled = false;
629|                             messageInput.placeholder = `Message pour ${fullUser.pseudo}...`;
630|                         }
631| 
632|                         messageInput.focus();
633|                         clearMessageArea();
634|                         loadHistory(fullUser.pseudo);
635| 
636|                         if (typeof window.showContactInfo === 'function') {
637|                             window.showContactInfo(
638|                                 fullUser.pseudo,
639|                                 fullUser.age,
640|                                 fullUser.origine,
641|                                 fullUser.gender,
642|                                 fullUser.tendencies,
643|                                 fullUser,
644|                                 fullUser.location_city || fullUser.location_code,
645|                                 fullUser.location_commune || null,
646|                                 fullUser.purpose,
647|                                 fullUser.bio || 'Pas de présentation',
648|                                 window.blockedUsers || []
649|                             );
650|                         }
651|                     };
652|                 }
653|                 usersDiv.appendChild(div);
654|             }
655| 
656|             const lastContact = sessionStorage.getItem('last_contact');
657|             if (lastContact) {
658|                 const userElements = document.querySelectorAll('.user-item');
659|                 for (let el of userElements) {
660|                     if (el.textContent.includes(lastContact) && !el.textContent.includes('(moi)') && el.style.background !== '#a8e3ee') {
661|                         el.click();
662|                         break;
663|                     }
664|                 }
665|             }
666| 
667|         } catch (err) {
668|             console.error('❌ Erreur chargement utilisateurs:', err);
669|         }
670|     };

723|                     reg.active.postMessage({ action: 'userConnected', connected: false });

745|     function updateUserBadge(warnsCount = 0) {
746|         const userBadge = document.getElementById('userBadge');
747|         if (!userBadge) return;
748| 
749|         // 🔥 Utiliser les nouveaux noms de colonnes
750|         let displayLocation = '?';
751| 
752| 
753|         if (user.location_city && user.location_city !== 'GPS' && user.location_city !== 'Me géolocaliser pour plus de précision') {
754|             displayLocation = user.location_city;
755|         } else {
756|             displayLocation = user.location_code || '?';
757|         }
758| 
759|         if (user.location_commune && user.location_commune !== 'null' &&
760|             user.location_commune !== 'undefined' && user.location_commune !== user.location_city) {
761|             displayLocation = `${user.location_commune} - ${displayLocation}`;
762|         }
763| 
764|         // Si on a une commune précise ET une ville différente, on les combine
765|         if (user.commune && user.commune !== 'null' &&
766|             user.commune !== 'undefined' && user.commune !== user.city) {
767|             displayLocation = `${user.commune} - ${displayLocation}`;
768|         }
769| 
770|         // Afficher le triangle seulement si warnsCount > 0
771|         if (warnsCount > 0) {
772|             const triangleColor = getTriangleColor(warnsCount);
773|             userBadge.innerHTML = `👤 ${escapeHtml(user.pseudo)} | ${escapeHtml(displayLocation)} <span style="color: ${triangleColor}; margin-left: 8px;">⚠️ ${warnsCount}</span>`;
774|         } else {
775|             userBadge.innerHTML = `👤 ${escapeHtml(user.pseudo)} | ${escapeHtml(displayLocation)}`;
776|         }
777|     }
778|

815|             if (e.target === settingsPanel) {

843|         if (warns >= 3) return '#ff0000';      // rouge
844|         if (warns === 2) return '#ff6600';     // orange foncé
845|         if (warns === 1) return '#ffaa00';     // orange clair
846|         return '#888888';                       // gris
847|     }

981|     // ENVOI ET RÉCEPTION DES MESSAGES

983|     socket.on('message sent confirmation', async (data) => {
984|         displayMessage(data.message, 'sent', null, currentTargetName);
985|         addToHistory(currentTargetName, data.message, 'sent', user.pseudo, currentTargetName);
986| 
987|         try {
988|             await fetch('/api/messages/save', {
989|                 method: 'POST',
990|                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
991|                 body: JSON.stringify({
992|                     from: user.pseudo,
993|                     to: currentTargetName,
994|                     message: data.message
995|                 })
996|             });
997|         } catch (err) {
998|             console.error('Erreur sauvegarde message:', err);
999|         }
1000| 
1001|         messageInput.value = '';
1002|         messageInput.focus();
1003|     });
1004| 
1005|     socket.on('private message', async (data) => {
1006|         const { toSocketId, message, fromPseudo } = data;
1007| 
1008|         // Vérifier si le destinataire est connecté
1009|         let targetSocketId = null;
1010|         try {
1011|             const result = await pool.query(
1012|                 'SELECT socket_id FROM users WHERE pseudo = $1 AND socket_id IS NOT NULL',
1013|                 [toSocketId]
1014|             );
1015|             if (result.rows.length > 0) {
1016|                 targetSocketId = result.rows[0].socket_id;
1017|             }
1018|         } catch (err) {
1019|             console.error('❌ Erreur recherche destinataire:', err.message);
1020|             socket.emit('error', { message: 'Erreur lors de l\'envoi du message' });
1021|             return;
1022|         }
1023| 
1024|         // Sauvegarder le message en base de données
1025|         try {
1026|             // 1. Vérifier si une conversation existe déjà entre les deux utilisateurs
1027|             const convResult = await pool.query(
1028|                 `SELECT id FROM conversations 
1029|              WHERE (user1_pseudo = $1 AND user2_pseudo = $2) 
1030|                 OR (user1_pseudo = $2 AND user2_pseudo = $1)`,
1031|                 [fromPseudo, toSocketId]
1032|             );
1033| 
1034|             let conversationId;
1035|             if (convResult.rows.length === 0) {
1036|                 // 2. Créer une nouvelle conversation
1037|                 const newConv = await pool.query(
1038|                     `INSERT INTO conversations (user1_pseudo, user2_pseudo, last_message, last_message_time) 
1039|                  VALUES ($1, $2, $3, NOW()) 
1040|                  RETURNING id`,
1041|                     [fromPseudo, toSocketId, message]
1042|                 );
1043|                 conversationId = newConv.rows[0].id;
1044|                 console.log(`📁 Nouvelle conversation ${conversationId} créée pour ${fromPseudo} ↔ ${toSocketId}`);
1045|             } else {
1046|                 conversationId = convResult.rows[0].id;
1047|                 // 3. Mettre à jour le dernier message
1048|                 await pool.query(
1049|                     `UPDATE conversations SET last_message = $1, last_message_time = NOW() WHERE id = $2`,
1050|                     [message, conversationId]
1051|                 );
1052|             }
1053| 
1054|             // 4. Insérer le message
1055|             await pool.query(
1056|                 `INSERT INTO messages (conversation_id, from_pseudo, to_pseudo, message, created_at) 
1057|              VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, NOW())`,
1058|                 [conversationId, fromPseudo, toSocketId, message]
1059|             );
1060|             console.log(`📦 Message sauvegardé dans la conversation ${conversationId}`);
1061| 
1062|         } catch (err) {
1063|             console.error('❌ Erreur sauvegarde message:', err.message);
1064|             // On continue même si la sauvegarde échoue, pour ne pas bloquer l'envoi
1065|         }
1066| 
1067|         // Si le destinataire est connecté, envoyer le message immédiatement
1068|         if (targetSocketId) {
1069|             io.to(targetSocketId).emit('private message received', {
1070|                 fromPseudo: fromPseudo,
1071|                 fromSocketId: socket.id,
1072|                 message: message,
1073|                 timestamp: new Date()
1074|             });
1075| 
1076|             // Confirmer à l'expéditeur
1077|             socket.emit('message sent confirmation', {
1078|                 message: message,
1079|                 toPseudo: toSocketId,
1080|                 timestamp: new Date()
1081|             });
1082| 
1083|             console.log(`💬 Message de ${fromPseudo} à ${toSocketId}: ${message.substring(0, 30)}...`);
1084|         } else {
1085|             // Le destinataire n'est pas connecté, le message est déjà sauvegardé en base
1086|             console.log(`💤 Message de ${fromPseudo} à ${toSocketId} sauvegardé (destinataire déconnecté)`);
1087|             socket.emit('message sent confirmation', {
1088|                 message: message,
1089|                 toPseudo: toSocketId,
1090|                 timestamp: new Date(),
1091|                 pending: true
1092|             });
1093|         }
1094|     });
1095| 
1096|     socket.on('error', (msg) => {
1097|         console.error('Erreur:', msg);
1098|     });
1099|

1176|         if (envelope.classList.contains('blink')) {
1177|             envelope.classList.remove('blink');
1178|         }

1181|         if (pendingMessages.length > 0) {
1182|             const msg = pendingMessages.shift();
1183|             displayMessage(msg.message, 'received', msg.fromPseudo);
1184|             addToHistory(msg.fromPseudo, msg.message, 'received', msg.fromPseudo, user.pseudo);
1185|             loadHistory(msg.fromPseudo);
1186|

1188|             currentTargetName = msg.fromPseudo;

1190|             sendBtn.disabled = false;

1192|             messageInput.focus();

1195|                 if (el.textContent.includes(msg.fromPseudo) && !el.textContent.includes('(moi)')) {
1196|                     document.querySelectorAll('.user-item').forEach(e => e.classList.remove('current-target'));
1197|                     el.classList.add('current-target');
1198|                 }
1199|             });
1200| 
1201|             const senderInfo = usersList.find(u => u.pseudo === msg.fromPseudo);
1202|             if (senderInfo) {
1203|                 let fullSender = senderInfo;
1204|                 if (typeof window.showContactInfo === 'function') {
1205|                     window.showContactInfo(
1206|                         fullSender.pseudo,
1207|                         fullSender.age,
1208|                         fullSender.origine,
1209|                         fullSender.gender,
1210|                         fullSender.tendencies,
1211|                         fullSender,
1212|                         fullSender.locationCity || fullSender.locationCode,
1213|                         fullSender.locationCommune || null,
1214|                         fullSender.purpose,
1215|                         fullSender.bio || 'Pas de présentation',
1216|                         window.blockedUsers || []
1217|                     );

1220|                 }

1223|                     window.showContactInfo(msg.fromPseudo, '?', '', '?', '?', {}, '?', null, '?', 'Bio non disponible', []);
1224|                 }
1225|             }
1226| 
1227|             updateEnvelope(pendingMessages.length);
1228|             if (pendingMessages.length === 0) {
1229|                 stopBlinking();
1230|             }
1231|         } else {
1232|             // 3️⃣ Sinon, ouvrir le chat normalement

1285|     function showPrivatePopup(pseudo) {
1286|         if (document.getElementById('privatePopup')) return;
1287|

1289|         if (!chatPanel) return;
1290| 
1291|         if (getComputedStyle(chatPanel).position !== 'relative') {
1292|             chatPanel.style.position = 'relative';
1293|         }
1294| 
1295|         const inputArea = document.querySelector('.input-area');
1296|         if (!inputArea) return;
1297| 
1298|         const inputRect = inputArea.getBoundingClientRect();
1299|         const chatRect = chatPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
1300|         const topPosition = inputRect.bottom - chatRect.top;
1301| 
1302|         const popup = document.createElement('div');
1303|         popup.id = 'privatePopup';
1304|         popup.style.cssText = `
1305|             position: absolute;
1306|             top: ${topPosition + 5}px;
1307|             left: 0;
1308|             right: 0;
1309|             background: #2c3e50;
1310|             color: white;
1311|             border-radius: 8px;
1312|             padding: 10px 12px;
1313|             z-index: 1000;
1314|             font-family: 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;
1315|             font-size: 12px;
1316|             display: flex;
1317|             justify-content: space-between;
1318|             align-items: center;
1319|             flex-wrap: wrap;
1320|             gap: 8px;
1321|             box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
1322|             opacity: 0;
1323|             transform: translateY(-10px);
1324|             transition: opacity 0.2s ease, transform 0.2s ease;
1325|         `;

1330|                 <button id="popupAccept" style="background:#27ae60; color:white; border:none; border-radius:4px; padding:4px 10px; cursor:pointer;">✅ Oui</button>
1331|                 <button id="popupDecline" style="background:#e74c3c; color:white; border:none; border-radius:4px; padding:4px 10px; cursor:pointer;">❌ Non</button>
1332|             </div>
1333|         `;

1458|         // Vérifier si le flag existe
1459|         const reloadFlag = sessionStorage.getItem('meetgay_is_reloading');
1460|         if (reloadFlag) {
1461|             console.log('✅ Flag de rechargement trouvé, on le nettoie');
1462|             // Nettoyer le flag APRÈS que la page est complètement chargée
1463|             setTimeout(() => {
1464|                 sessionStorage.removeItem('meetgay_is_reloading');
1465|                 isPageReloading = false;
1466|                 console.log('✅ Flag de rechargement nettoyé');
1467|             }, 1500); // Attendre 1.5s pour laisser le temps à initUser() de s'exécuter
1468|         }
1469|     });

1471|     // 3. GESTION DU RETOUR ARRIÈRE (popstate)
1472|     window.addEventListener('popstate', function (event) {
1473|         console.log('🔔 popstate déclenché - Vérification en cours...');
1474| 
1475|         // VÉRIFICATION 1 : Flag de rechargement
1476|         const reloadFlag = sessionStorage.getItem('meetgay_is_reloading');
1477|         const isReloading = reloadFlag !== null || isPageReloading;
1478| 
1479|         // VÉRIFICATION 2 : Performance API (moderne)
1480|         let isReload = false;
1481|         try {
1482|             const navEntries = performance.getEntriesByType('navigation');
1483|             if (navEntries && navEntries.length > 0) {
1484|                 isReload = navEntries[0].type === 'reload';
1485|                 console.log(`📊 Type de navigation: ${navEntries[0].type}`);
1486|             }
1487|         } catch (e) {
1488|             // Fallback pour les vieux navigateurs
1489|             if (window.performance && window.performance.navigation) {
1490|                 isReload = window.performance.navigation.type === 1;
1491|                 console.log(`📊 Navigation type (fallback): ${window.performance.navigation.type}`);
1492|             }
1493|         }
1494| 
1495|         // SI C'EST UN RECHARGEMENT, ON IGNORE TOUT
1496|         if (isReloading || isReload) {
1497|             console.log(`🔄 IGNORÉ: isReloading=${isReloading}, isReload=${isReload}`);
1498|             return;
1499|         }
1500| 
1501|         // --- ICI, C'EST UN VRAI RETOUR ARRIÈRE ---
1502|         console.log('⬅️ RETOUR ARRIÈRE DÉTECTÉ');
1503| 
1504|         // Récupérer le pseudo
1505|         const storedUser = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('meetgay_user') || '{}');
1506|         const pseudo = storedUser.pseudo || currentUserPseudo;
1507| 
1508|         if (!pseudo) {
1509|             console.warn('⚠️ Aucun pseudo trouvé pour le retour arrière');
1510|             window.location.href = '/login.html';
1511|             return;
1512|         }
1513| 
1514|         console.log(`🗑️ Suppression de ${pseudo} (retour arrière)`);
1515|         const data = JSON.stringify({ pseudo: pseudo });
1516| 
1517|         // Supprimer de la base (avec sendBeacon)
1518|         navigator.sendBeacon('/api/mark-for-deletion', data);
1519|         setTimeout(() => navigator.sendBeacon('/api/delete-user', data), 50);
1520|         setTimeout(() => navigator.sendBeacon('/api/messages/delete-user', data), 100);
1521| 
1522|         // Nettoyer le localStorage et sessionStorage
1523|         localStorage.removeItem('meetgay_user');
1524|         localStorage.removeItem('meetgay_user_full');
1525|         localStorage.removeItem('meetgay_token');
1526|         localStorage.removeItem('meetgay_chat_history');
1527|         sessionStorage.removeItem('last_contact');
1528|         sessionStorage.removeItem('conversationHistory');
1529|         sessionStorage.removeItem('meetgay_is_reloading');
1530| 
1531|         // Rediriger vers login
1532|         setTimeout(() => {
1533|             window.location.href = '/login.html';
1534|         }, 200);
1535|     });

# FILE: public\js\common.js
# ============================================

1| ﻿// ========== COMMON - MeetGay ==========
2| // Fonctions et détecteurs partagés entre les pages (login, chat, etc.)
3| 
4| (function () {
5|     // ========== 1. Détection de la résolution d'écran ==========
6|     function detectResolution() {
7|         const width = window.innerWidth;
8|         const height = window.innerHeight;
9|         const resolution = `${width}x${height}`;
10|         sessionStorage.setItem('screen_resolution', resolution);
11|         console.log(`📱 Résolution détectée : ${resolution}`);
12|         return resolution;
13|     }
14| 
15|     // ========== 2. Échapper le HTML (sécurité) ==========
16|     function escapeHtml(str) {
17|         if (!str) return '';
18|         return str.replace(/[&<>]/g, (m) => {
19|             if (m === '&') return '&amp;';
20|             if (m === '<') return '&lt;';
21|             if (m === '>') return '&gt;';
22|             return m;
23|         });
24|     }
25| 
26|     // ========== 3. Formater l'heure (HH:MM) ==========
27|     function formatTime(date) {
28|         if (!date) return '';
29|         const d = new Date(date);
30|         return `${d.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${d.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
31|     }
32| 
33|     // ========== 4. Exécuter la détection au chargement ==========
34|     if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
35|         document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', detectResolution);
36|     } else {
37|         detectResolution();
38|     }
39| 
40|     // ========== 5. Exposer les fonctions globalement ==========
41|     window.common = {
42|         escapeHtml: escapeHtml,
43|         formatTime: formatTime,
44|         detectResolution: detectResolution,
45|         getResolution: () => sessionStorage.getItem('screen_resolution')
46|     };
47| 
48|     // Fonction pour afficher la localisation de manière cohérente (utilisée par le badge ET la liste des connectés)
49|     window.getDisplayLocation = function (userData) {
50|         if (!userData) return '?';
51| 
52|         let locationCity = userData.locationCity || userData.location_city || null;
53|         let locationCommune = userData.locationCommune || userData.location_commune || null;
54|         let locationCode = userData.locationCode || userData.location_code || '';
55| 
56|         // Si on a une commune précise différente de la ville, on combine
57|         if (locationCommune && locationCommune !== 'null' && locationCommune !== 'undefined' && locationCommune !== locationCity) {
58|             return `${locationCommune} - ${locationCity}`;
59|         }
60| 
61|         // Sinon on affiche juste la ville
62|         if (locationCity && locationCity !== 'null' && locationCity !== 'undefined') {
63|             return locationCity;
64|         }
65| 
66|         // Fallback : code de la liste déroulante
67|         if (locationCode && locationCode !== 'null' && locationCode !== 'undefined' && locationCode !== 'GPS') {
68|             return locationCode;
69|         }
70| 
71|         return '?';
72|     };
73| })();

1| ﻿import FingerprintJS from '@fingerprintjs/fingerprintjs';
2| 
3| (async () => {
4|     try {
5|         const fp = await FingerprintJS.load();
6|         const result = await fp.get();
7|         const visitorId = result.visitorId;
8|         sessionStorage.setItem('fingerprint', visitorId);
9|         window.currentFingerprint = visitorId;
10|         console.log('🔒 Fingerprint généré:', visitorId);
11|     } catch (err) {
12|         console.error('❌ Erreur fingerprint:', err);
13|         // Fallback temporaire (évite de bloquer l'utilisateur)
14|         const fallback = 'temp_' + Date.now();
15|         sessionStorage.setItem('fingerprint', fallback);
16|         window.currentFingerprint = fallback;
17|     }
18| })();

81|     // 2. Fonction de géolocalisation
82|     function startGeolocation() {
83|         if (!navigator.geolocation) {
84|             gpsStatus.innerHTML = '❌ Votre navigateur ne supporte pas la géolocalisation. Utilisez la liste déroulante.';
85|             return;
86|         }
87| 
88|         navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
89|             // Succès
90|             function (position) {
91|                 detectedLocationLat = position.coords.latitude;
92|                 detectedLocationLng = position.coords.longitude;
93| 
94|                 gpsStatus.innerHTML = '📍 Position trouvée ! Je cherche le nom de la ville...';
95| 
96|                 // Appel à Nominatim pour avoir le nom de la ville
97|                 getCityName(detectedLocationLat, detectedLocationLng);
98| 
99|             },
100|             // Erreur
101|             function (error) {
102|                 let msg = '❌ Erreur de géolocalisation : ';
103|                 switch (error.code) {
104|                     case error.PERMISSION_DENIED:
105|                         msg += 'Vous avez refusé l\'accès à votre position. Utilisez la liste déroulante.';
106|                         break;
107|                     case error.POSITION_UNAVAILABLE:
108|                         msg += 'Position indisponible. Vérifiez votre connexion.';
109|                         break;
110|                     case error.TIMEOUT:
111|                         msg += 'Temps écoulé. Réessayez.';
112|                         break;
113|                     default:
114|                         msg += error.message;
115|                 }
116|                 gpsStatus.innerHTML = msg;
117|             },
118|             {
119|                 enableHighAccuracy: true,
120|                 timeout: 10000,
121|                 maximumAge: 0
122|             }
123|         );
124|     }
125| 
126|     function getCityName(lat, lng) {
127|         const url = `/api/geocode?lat=${lat}&lng=${lng}`;
128| 
129|         fetch(url)
130|             .then(response => response.json())
131|             .then(data => {
132|                 console.log('📡 Réponse Google Maps:', data);
133| 
134|                 if (data.status === 'OK' && data.results.length > 0) {
135|                     // On prend le premier résultat (le plus précis)
136|                     // === NOUVEAU CODE ===
137|                     // 🔥 On prend le résultat le PLUS PRÉCIS (viewport le plus petit)
138|                     const sortedResults = [...data.results].sort((a, b) => {
139|                         const sizeA = (a.geometry.viewport.northeast.lat - a.geometry.viewport.southwest.lat) *
140|                             (a.geometry.viewport.northeast.lng - a.geometry.viewport.southwest.lng);
141|                         const sizeB = (b.geometry.viewport.northeast.lat - b.geometry.viewport.southwest.lat) *
142|                             (b.geometry.viewport.northeast.lng - b.geometry.viewport.southwest.lng);
143|                         return sizeA - sizeB;
144|                     });
145|                     const result = sortedResults[0];
146|                     console.log('📍 Résultat le PLUS PRÉCIS sélectionné:', result.formatted_address);
147| 
148|                     // --- RÉCUPÉRATION DES COMPOSANTS ---
149|                     const components = result.address_components;
150|                     console.log('📡 COMPOSANTS COMPLETS:', components.map(c => ({ long_name: c.long_name, types: c.types })));
151| 
152|                     // --- RÉCUPÉRATION DES COMPOSANTS ---
153|                     const trouver = (type) => components.find(c => c.types.includes(type))?.long_name;
154|                     // ... le reste du code
155| 
156|                     const locality = trouver("locality") || trouver("postal_town");
157|                     const sublocality = trouver("sublocality") || trouver("sublocality_level_1") || trouver("neighborhood");
158|                     const postalCode = trouver("postal_code");
159|                     const province = trouver("administrative_area_level_2") || trouver("administrative_area_level_3");
160|                     const region = trouver("administrative_area_level_1");
161|                     const country = trouver("country");
162|                     const neighborhood = trouver("neighborhood") || sublocality;
163| 
164|                     // 🔥 NOUVEAU : Récupérer les localités du code postal
165|                     const postcodeLocalities = data.results.find(r => r.types.includes("postal_code"))?.postcode_localities || [];
166| 
167|                     console.log('📍 Locality:', locality);
168|                     console.log('📍 Sublocality:', sublocality);
169|                     console.log('📍 Postcode Localities:', postcodeLocalities);
170| 
171|                     // --- DÉTERMINATION DE LA COMMUNE PRÉCISE ---
172|                     let communePrecise = locality;
173| 
174|                     // Priorité 1: sublocality (Pamel, Borchtlombeek, Strijtem)
175|                     if (sublocality && sublocality !== locality) {
176|                         communePrecise = sublocality;
177|                     }
178|                     // Priorité 2: postcode_localities (si sublocality n'existe pas)
179|                     else if (postcodeLocalities.length > 0 && postcodeLocalities[0] !== locality) {
180|                         communePrecise = postcodeLocalities[0];
181|                     }
182| 
183|                     console.log('✅ Commune précise trouvée:', communePrecise);
184| 
185|                     // --- AFFICHAGE ---
186|                     gpsStatus.style.display = 'none';
187|                     gpsResult.style.display = 'block';
188|                     detectedCity.textContent = locality;
189|                     document.getElementById('detectedCounty').textContent = communePrecise;
190| 
191|                     // OPTION 1 : juste la ville
192|                     document.getElementById('choiceRegionLabel').textContent = locality;
193| 
194|                     // OPTION 2 : commune précise + ville (si différente)
195|                     let option2 = locality;
196|                     if (communePrecise && communePrecise !== locality) {
197|                         option2 = `${communePrecise} - ${locality}`;
198|                     } else if (detectedLocationCountry) {
199|                         option2 = `${locality} - ${detectedLocationCountry}`;
200|                     }
201|                     document.getElementById('choicePreciseLabel').textContent = option2;
202| 
203|                     // Réinitialiser les radios
204|                     document.querySelectorAll('input[name="locationChoice"]').forEach(el => el.checked = false);
205|                     document.getElementById('gpsConsent').checked = false;
206|                     document.getElementById('gpsConfirm').disabled = true;
207|                     gpsConsent.disabled = false;
208| 
209|                     // Stocker pour confirmation
210|                     detectedLocationCity = locality;
211|                     detectedLocationCommune = communePrecise;
212|                     detectedLocationPostalCode = postalCode || '';
213|                     detectedLocationProvince = province || '';
214|                     detectedLocationRegion = region || '';
215|                     detectedLocationCountry = country || '';
216|                     detectedLocationNeighborhood = sublocality || neighborhood || '';
217|                     detectedLocationPostcodeLocalities = postcodeLocalities.join(', ') || '';
218|                     detectedLocationLat = lat;
219|                     detectedLocationLng = lng;
220| 
221|                     // Message informatif
222|                     const infoMsg = document.createElement('div');
223|                     infoMsg.style.cssText = 'font-size: 0.8em; color: #666; margin-top: 5px;';
224|                     infoMsg.innerHTML = `
225|                     ℹ️ <strong>Option 1:</strong> ${locality}<br>
226|                     ℹ️ <strong>Option 2:</strong> ${option2}
227|                 `;
228|                     const oldMsg = document.querySelector('.gps-info-msg');
229|                     if (oldMsg) oldMsg.remove();
230|                     infoMsg.className = 'gps-info-msg';
231|                     document.querySelector('#gpsResult')?.appendChild(infoMsg);
232| 
233|                 } else {
234|                     gpsStatus.innerHTML = '❌ Erreur Google Maps';
235|                 }
236|             })
237|             .catch(error => {
238|                 gpsStatus.innerHTML = '❌ Erreur';
239|                 console.error(error);
240|             });
241|     }
242| 
243|     // 4. Case à cocher : activer/désactiver le bouton Confirmer
244|     gpsConsent.addEventListener('change', function () {

251|         radio.addEventListener('change', function () {
252|             const consentChecked = gpsConsent.checked;
253|             gpsConfirm.disabled = !(consentChecked && this.checked);
254|         });
255|     });
256| 
257|     // 5. Bouton Confirmer
258|     gpsConfirm.addEventListener('click', function () {
259|         if (!detectedLocationCity) {
260|             alert('Aucune ville détectée. Réessayez.');
261|             return;
262|         }
263| 
264|         const selectedChoice = document.querySelector('input[name="locationChoice"]:checked');
265|         if (!selectedChoice) {
266|             alert('Veuillez choisir comment afficher votre localité.');
267|             return;
268|         }
269| 
270|         let locationCity = '';
271|         let locationCommune = null;
272| 
273|         // Créer les champs cachés pour toutes les données de localisation
274|         function createHiddenField(id, value) {
275|             let field = document.getElementById(id);
276|             if (!field) {
277|                 field = document.createElement('input');
278|                 field.type = 'hidden';
279|                 field.id = id;
280|                 field.name = id.replace('_hidden', '');
281|                 document.getElementById('loginForm').appendChild(field);
282|             }
283|             field.value = value || '';
284|         }
285| 
286| 
287|         if (selectedChoice.value === 'region_only') {
288|             locationCity = detectedLocationCity;
289|             locationCommune = null;
290|         } else {
291|             if (detectedLocationCommune && detectedLocationCommune !== detectedLocationCity) {
292|                 locationCity = detectedLocationCity;
293|                 locationCommune = detectedLocationCommune;
294|             } else {
295|                 locationCity = detectedLocationCity;
296|                 locationCommune = null;
297|             }
298|         }
299| 
300|         // Stocker toutes les données dans des champs cachés
301|         createHiddenField('location_city_hidden', locationCity);
302|         createHiddenField('location_commune_hidden', locationCommune);
303|         createHiddenField('location_postal_code_hidden', detectedLocationPostalCode);
304|         createHiddenField('location_province_hidden', detectedLocationProvince);
305|         createHiddenField('location_region_hidden', detectedLocationRegion);
306|         createHiddenField('location_country_hidden', detectedLocationCountry);
307|         createHiddenField('location_neighborhood_hidden', detectedLocationNeighborhood);
308|         createHiddenField('location_postcode_localities_hidden', detectedLocationPostcodeLocalities);
309|         createHiddenField('location_sublocality_level_1_hidden', detectedLocationSublocalityLevel1);
310|         createHiddenField('location_sublocality_level_2_hidden', detectedLocationSublocalityLevel2);
311|         createHiddenField('location_sublocality_level_3_hidden', detectedLocationSublocalityLevel3);
312|         createHiddenField('location_sublocality_level_4_hidden', detectedLocationSublocalityLevel4);
313|         createHiddenField('location_sublocality_level_5_hidden', detectedLocationSublocalityLevel5);
314|         createHiddenField('location_admin_level_3_hidden', detectedLocationAdminLevel3);
315|         createHiddenField('location_admin_level_4_hidden', detectedLocationAdminLevel4);
316|         createHiddenField('location_admin_level_5_hidden', detectedLocationAdminLevel5);
317|         createHiddenField('location_admin_level_6_hidden', detectedLocationAdminLevel6);
318|         createHiddenField('location_admin_level_7_hidden', detectedLocationAdminLevel7);
319|         createHiddenField('location_colloquial_area_hidden', detectedLocationColloquialArea);
320|         createHiddenField('location_intersection_hidden', detectedLocationIntersection);
321|         createHiddenField('location_lat_hidden', detectedLocationLat);
322|         createHiddenField('location_lng_hidden', detectedLocationLng);
323| 
324|         // 🔥 DEBUG - Vérifier les valeurs
325|         console.log('📍 Sauvegarde GPS - locationCity:', locationCity);
326|         console.log('📍 Sauvegarde GPS - locationCommune:', locationCommune);
327| 
328| 
329|         // 🔥 On met la VILLE dans le select, pas "GPS"
330|         // On crée une option pour la ville si elle n'existe pas
331|         const locationSelect = document.getElementById('location');
332|         let optionExists = false;
333|         for (let opt of locationSelect.options) {
334|             if (opt.value === locationCity) {
335|                 optionExists = true;
336|                 break;
337|             }
338|         }
339|         if (!optionExists) {
340|             const newOption = document.createElement('option');
341|             newOption.value = locationCity;
342|             newOption.textContent = locationCity;
343|             locationSelect.appendChild(newOption);
344|         }
345|         locationSelect.value = locationCity;
346| 
347|         const displayText = locationCommune ? `${locationCommune} - ${locationCity}` : locationCity;
348| 
349|         gpsZone.innerHTML = `
350|         <div style="padding: 10px; background: #e8f5e9; border-radius: 5px; color: #2e7d32;">
351|             ✅ Localisation confirmée : <strong>${locationCity}</strong>
352|             <br><small>Votre profil affichera : ${displayText}</small>
353|         </div>
354|     `;
355|     });
356| 
357| 
358|     // 6. Bouton Annuler / Reset
359|     gpsCancel.addEventListener('click', function () {
360|         // Remettre le select sur une valeur vide
361|         locationSelect.value = '';
362|         locationSelect.disabled = false;
363| 
364|         // Cacher la zone GPS
365|         gpsZone.style.display = 'none';
366|         gpsResult.style.display = 'none';
367|         gpsStatus.style.display = 'block';
368|         gpsConsent.checked = false;
369|         gpsConsent.disabled = true;
370|         gpsConfirm.disabled = true;
371| 
372| 
373|         detectedLocationCity = '';
374|         detectedLocationCommune = '';
375|         detectedLocationPostalCode = '';
376|         detectedLocationProvince = '';
377|         detectedLocationRegion = '';
378|         detectedLocationCountry = '';
379|         detectedLocationNeighborhood = '';
380|         detectedLocationPostcodeLocalities = '';
381|         detectedLocationSublocalityLevel1 = '';
382|         detectedLocationSublocalityLevel2 = '';
383|         detectedLocationSublocalityLevel3 = '';
384|         detectedLocationSublocalityLevel4 = '';
385|         detectedLocationSublocalityLevel5 = '';
386|         detectedLocationAdminLevel3 = '';
387|         detectedLocationAdminLevel4 = '';
388|         detectedLocationAdminLevel5 = '';
389|         detectedLocationAdminLevel6 = '';
390|         detectedLocationAdminLevel7 = '';
391|         detectedLocationColloquialArea = '';
392|         detectedLocationIntersection = '';
393|         detectedLocationLat = null;
394|         detectedLocationLng = null;
395|     });
396| 
397|     // Réinitialiser les radios
398|     document.querySelectorAll('input[name="locationChoice"]').forEach(el => el.checked = false);

404|     // ===== INITIALISATION DE L'AUTOCOMPLÉTION =====
405|     async function initAutocomplete() {
406|         try {
407|             if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.maps) {
408|                 console.log('⏳ Google Maps non chargé, réessai dans 500ms');
409|                 setTimeout(initAutocomplete, 500);
410|                 return;
411|             }
412| 
413|             const { AutocompleteSuggestion } = await google.maps.importLibrary("places");
414|             autocompleteService = AutocompleteSuggestion;
415|             sessionToken = new google.maps.places.AutocompleteSessionToken();
416|             console.log('✅ Autocomplétion initialisée (Places New)');
417|         } catch (err) {
418|             console.error('❌ Erreur init autocomplete:', err);
419|         }
420|     }
